# Domain and Product semantics — accepted and candidate (survey view)

Classification: internal, redacted, non-normative survey evidence. Not authority. Not for publication.
Generated mechanically from `raw/surveyors/*/entries.jsonl` (surveyor-read items only unless stated). Verbatim cells are truncated; the JSONL is the full record.

Grouping is by keyword match on `subject`/`statement`; an item may appear under several terms. Standing is as recorded by the surveyor; nothing here is ratified by this survey.

| S61D-E004 | principle_candidate | Principal-accepted | Wheelwright's interpretation of 'Don't make me think': the system performs and remembers routine decision reconstruction; operator cognition is reserved for genuine judgment. | Do not make the operator perform unnecessary decision reconstruction that the system can perform and remember.
